2026.09.01

1319.TT 東陽 
 邁入傳統旺季 營運逐季回升

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
公司主要營業項目為汽 、 機車零組件 ， 為台灣塑膠件供應商 ， 也是全球第一大汽車AM
塑膠件製造商。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 100 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，100 
【大盤指數/股價】46128 / 82.6

➢ 2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業
費用為 9.5 億元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期
的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36 元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優
於預期。2Q26 營收 56.63 億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36
元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
➢ 3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%，使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現。
目前客戶之庫存水位約在一個月左右，而越靠近北半球之 AM 需求旺季
時，客戶有將庫存備至 2~3 個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，
因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升至 95%左右之水準 ， 同事帶動
毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長 。2026 上半年之 AM 件月營收約落在 14.4
億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收能提升
至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續
有望以此為低標向上成長 。OEM 方面 ， 台灣約佔32%， 中國則佔約68%，
台灣市場預計將微幅成長 3%，中國則估計衰退個位數百分比。 
總體而言，受惠 2026 下半年邁入傳統 AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季
成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元， QoQ+4.15%，
YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
➢ 股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長，公司持續開發的模具數量
也成為高度的競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進
行競爭。研究員預估 2026 年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，
YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29 元，YoY-6.53% / +4.65%。
當前評價約位於 12 倍，位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣位置，當前預
估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

個股報告

公司介紹 
東陽成立於 1967 年，主要營業項目為汽機車零組件，為台灣塑膠件供應商，也是全
球第一大汽車 AM 塑膠件製造商，AM 塑膠件市占率約達 70%，而 AM 鈑金件全球
市佔率約 38%。除了在台南本廠有生產基地外，在桃園、中國大陸、美國、義大利等
地共設有 25 座生產據點。公司產品項目多元，包括引擎蓋、儀表板、車內飾板、行
李箱蓋、擾流板、前後保險桿、水箱護罩、水箱支架等塑料/鈑金件，產品多元使公司
可提供全球 85%~90%的碰撞零組件，約當超過 25,000 個品項，且每年持續投入約
18 億元之模具開發費用。產品營收佔比方面，AM 營收佔比約 75%、OEM 營收佔比
約 25%。以 AM 產品組合畫分來看，其中以保險桿為大宗，約佔 40%，水箱護罩、
水箱支架、葉子鈑、引擎蓋等分別佔 14%、3%、10%、11%。OEM 產品組合則以保
險桿為主，約佔 31%，儀錶板、水箱護罩、模具分別佔 17%、3%、7%。 
圖 1：東陽 AM 件產品項目

資料來源：東陽公司資料 
2Q26 財報符合預期 
回顧 2Q26 營運概況，毛利率為 32.5%，符合原先預期的 32.4%。營業費用為 9.5 億
元，較預期的 9.78 億元低。營益率為 15.76%，優於預期的 15.2%。EPS 則為 1.36
元，優於預期的 1.21 元，整體獲利率小幅優於預期。關稅在非半導體 232 關稅優惠
措施實施前，客戶拉貨仍面臨 27.5%之關稅，使庫存回補力道減弱，而 2Q 本來就為
該產業的營運淡季，庫存回補需求持續偏弱，導致 4 月、5 月、6 月營收持續呈現月
減之狀態 ， 預估2Q26 為全年營運谷底 ， 後續獲利將穩步向上回升 。2Q26 營收 56.63
億元，QoQ+0.57%，YoY-3.92%。EPS 1.36 元，QoQ-15.58%，YoY+76.7%。 
3Q26 關稅已下滑至 15% 客戶庫存回補逐漸顯現 
隨著關稅大幅自 27.5%下滑至 15%， 使客戶對庫存回補需求逐漸顯現 。 目前客戶之庫
存水位約在一個月左右， 而越靠近北半球之AM 需求旺季時 ，客戶有將庫存備至2~3
個月之需求，時間點約落在 9~10 月之間，因此產能利用率屆時將自當前的 90%提升
至 95%左右之水準，同事帶動毛利率因經濟規模提升而成長。2026 上半年之 AM 件
月營收約落在 14.4 億元上下，鑒於邁入 2026 下半年營運旺季，期望 AM 件月營收
能提升至 15.5 億元左右之水準，當前 7 月之 AM 件營收已達 15.5 億元，後續有望以
此為低標向上成長。OEM 方面，台灣約佔 32%，中國則佔約 68%，台灣市場預計將

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
微幅成長 3%， 中國則估計衰退個位數百分比 。 總體而言 ， 受惠2026 下半年邁入傳統
AM 件旺季，營收有望呈現逐季成長之態勢。研究員預估 3Q26 營收 58.98 億元，
QoQ+4.15%，YoY+9.19%。EPS 1.44 元，QoQ+5.27%，YoY+29.3%。 
長期 AM 件仍將逐漸轉往 OEM 件 使東陽可觸及市場穩步擴大 
展望未來，AM 市場持續在侵蝕 OEM 市場，以台灣而言，碰撞更換零組件市場中以
AM 為大宗，約佔 80%，OEM 則約佔 20%。美國使用 AM 件的佔比也從過去的個位
數%提升到目前的 21%，估計在 2030 年可達到 28%左右之水準。歐洲則從過去的
20+%提升到 30%。主因歐美地區的保險公司為了降低支出成本，持續鼓勵客戶選擇
使用品質與 OEM 件相近的 AM 件，一方面保險公司可以降低支出成本，另一方面被
保險人可以降低他的保費支出，達成雙贏的局面，也是AM 件在全球的佔比能持續穩
步提升的主要原因 。 因此在AM 件滲透率持續提升的狀況下 ， 公司每年的AM 件營收
都能保有 5%~8%的穩定成長，屬於穩健成長的經營型態。 
在競爭優勢方面，公司模具數量多，可提供超過 25,000 個品項，涵蓋了全球 80%在
路上跑的車型，且模具開發成本高、開發時間長(平均需約 6~8 個月)，東陽每年持續
開發 300~400 套模具，形成了高度的進入門檻。使AM 件的市佔率能維持在 70%以
上的高市佔率表現 。 除此之外 ， 保險公司理賠的AM 件還必須通過 CAPA 認證 ，CAPA
為美國獨立認證組織(Certified Automotive Parts Association) ，通過認證的零件表
示其品質與 OEM 件相同或是更高，在歐美市場更受到保險公司(如 State Farm)及消
費者的信任。 
股價回檔為長期買入點 評價偏低 維持買進建議  
考量 AM 件之整體市場規模每年仍持續成長 ， 公司持續開發的模具數量也成為高度的
競爭障礙，估計短期難有取代公司的 AM 件碰撞廠商來進行競爭。研究員預估 2026
年/2027 年營收 233.31 億元/249.15 億元，YoY-7.03% / +6.79%，EPS 6.01 元/6.29
元，YoY-6.53%/+4.65%。當前評價約位於 12 倍， 位於過往區間12X~23X 的中下緣
位置，當前預估殖利率約 5.6%，具下檔殖利率保護，維持買進投資建議，目標價100
元(2027F PE 16X)。

ABOUT ETR
Enterprise Technology Research (ETR) is an enterprise technology market research firm that delivers
actionable, transparent, and unbiased insights to institutional investors, drawing on proprietary surveys of
a trusted community of nearly 10,000 technology leaders. For more than 15 years, ETR's data has

empowered its clients to make smarter, faster decisions. ETR is a Futurum Group company. Learn more
at etr.ai.
ABOUT THE FUTURUM GROUP
The Futurum Group is a technology research, intelligence, and advisory firm. Futurum Intelligence, the
research arm of The Futurum Group, is led by analysts, researchers, and advisors who help business
leaders worldwide anticipate tectonic shifts in their industries and leverage disruptive innovation. Learn
more at futurumgroup.com.

Total datacenter capex, AI and general purpose. Shares may not sum to 100 because of rounding.
Capex per gigawatt is set to increase: stable at
$35B to $36B in prior years, but set to increase over

10% this year, driven by higher component costs.
Memory repricing: system DRAM goes from 7% of
datacenter capex in 2025 to 20% in 2026, and is
unlikely to come down materially.
CPUs are a smaller moving part: set to increase
from $64B this year to over $250B in 2030, driven
primarily by standalone deployments.
Total capex can exceed $4T in 2030:2.8x 2026
spending, with close to $1.3T of it on accelerators.

1 群益投顧 
市 場 流 傳 一 份 疑 似 台 達 電(2308 TT) 發 給 客 戶 的 通 知 指 出，特 定 批 次 電 源 供 應 器 存 在 潛
在 品 質 問 題 。 此 次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件 ， 並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計 ， 且 台 達 電 擁 有
不 同 電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決， 影 響 有 限。 台達電
為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商 ， 受 惠 於AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動 AC/DC 電 源 大 幅
成長外， CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC 電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成
長動能 ， 投 資 評 等 為 Buy ，目標價為 2,050 元。

研 究 員 ： 王 美 珍   
iris.wang@capital.com.tw

電 容 器 更 換 事 件 對 營 運 影 響 有 限：  
市場流傳一份疑似台達 電 發給客戶的通知指出，特定批次電源供應器存在
潛 在 品 質 問 題 。內 容 表 示 根 據 調 查 ， 受 影 響 PSU 所 使 用 的 部 分 電 容 器 ， 在
未 施 加 電 壓、長 時 間 存 放 後，可 能 出 現 漏 電 流 增 加 情 況，特 定 條 件 下 恐 進 一
步 造 成 電 容 器 電 壓 不 平 衡 及 過 熱，重 新 上 電 時 可 能 導 致 電 容 器 故 障。此 次 涉
及台達 電 新一代 VR 平台 18.3 kW PSU ， 相 關 問 題 於 試 產 階 段 發 現 ， 台 達電
隨 後 通 知 客 戶 並 進 行 電 容 器 更 換 。 
台達 電 表 示 不 評 論 市 場 傳 聞；若 相 關 情 況 確 認 屬 供 應 商 零 組 件 問 題，公 司 將
依 實 際 狀 況 更 換 供 應 商 ， 並 配 合 客 戶 需 求 進 行 後 續 處 理 。 
此次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件，並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計，且 台 達電 擁 有 不 同
電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商， 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動
AC/DC 電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC
電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能 。受 惠 於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ，
營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ， 投 資 評 等 為Buy ， 目 標 價 為 2, 050 元。

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

廣 達 電 腦(Quanta) 為 全 球 筆 記 型 電 腦 專 業 研 發 設 計 製 造 與 雲 端 運
算 解 決 方 案 領 導 供 應 商 ，為 全 球 前 五 大 筆 記 型 電 腦 代 工 廠 商 。 公
司 主 要 替 品 牌 業 者 從 事 代 工 服 務 ， 代 工 項 目 包 含 筆 記 型 電 腦 、 平
板 電 腦 、 智 慧 型 手 機 、 伺 服 器 、 商 用 及 家 用 智 慧 物 聯 網 裝 置 、 消
費 性 電 子 產 品 、 車 用 電 子 等 。 公 司 總 部 位 於 桃 園 龜 山 ， 生 產 基 地
包 含 桃 園 、 大 陸 上 海 、 重 慶 、 江 蘇 、 美 國 田 納 西 州 、 加 州 、 墨 西
哥 、 德 國 、 泰 國 等 。 公 司2025 年 伺 服 器 業 務(包含 AI 伺服器 )佔
整 體 營 收 比 重 近80% ， 筆 電(PC) 及 其 他 業 務 佔 約20% 。202 5 年
銷 售 地 區 比 重 ： 美 國73%、 大 陸 5%、 荷 蘭 2%、 日 本 3%、 其 他
17%。 筆 記 型 電 腦 主 要 客 戶 為Apple 、HP、Dell 、 華 碩 、 宏 碁 ，
伺 服 器 客 戶 包 含Microsoft 、Google 、Amazon 、Meta ， 而 車 用 客
戶為 Tesla 、GM 。 主 要 競 爭 對 手 為 鴻 海 、 緯 創 、 和 碩 、 仁 寶 電 腦
及 英 業 達 等 。

二. 營運概況及展望  
圖 一 、 廣 達 製 造 基 地 及 全 球 據 點

資 料 來 源 ：廣 達 、 宏 遠 投 顧 彙 整

1. 四大 CSP 上修 今年 資本支出達 7,450 億美元  
在 生 成 式AI 商 機 快 速 擴 張 帶 動 下，全 球 雲 端 基 礎 設 施 投 資 進 入 新
一 輪 高 峰 。 亞 馬 遜 、Alphabet 和 微 軟 認 為 其 在 先 前 的 鉅 額 投 資 已
帶 動 營 收 明 顯 成 長 ， 同 時 預 估 未 來AI 潛 在 商 機 仍 持 續 擴 大 。 其 中，
亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 已 陸 續 上 調2026 全 年 投 資 金 額，且 四
大雲端服務供應商 (CSP )總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新高
規模 。 亞 馬 遜 、 微 軟 、Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分別
為 1,306 億、1,701 億 美 元，同 時 亞 馬 遜、Alphabet 和 Meta 再上
修 2026 全 年 資 本 支 出 金 額，2026 年 資 本 支 出預 估 將達 7,450 億
美元 。而 根據 TrendForce 最 新 產 業 研 究，全 球 主 要 雲 端 服 務 供 應
商(CSP) 為 加 速 建 置AI 基 礎 設 施 ， 資 本 支 出 預 估 於2026 年 將 年
增 98%，2027 年 再 成 長50% 至 1.3 兆 美 元 左 右，年 增 率 收 斂的 主
要 原因， 除了 反 映 高 基 期 因 素之外 ， 部 分 可 歸 咎 為鉅 額 資 本 支 出
對 四 大 CSP 財 務 衝 擊 日 益 擴 大。然 而， 在 AI Inference 、Agentic 
AI 、 自 研 ASIC 和 新 一 代AI 模 型 持 續 推 升 算 力 需 求 的 情 況 下 ， 預
期 整 體 CSP 投 資 金 額 將 續 創 新 高 紀 錄 ， 產 業 成 長 有 望 從 原 先 以
GPU 擴 張 為 主，轉 為 包 含AI server 、液 冷 散 熱、先 進 封 裝、高 速
互 聯 、 電 源 和 記 憶 體 等 基 礎 設 施 全 面 升 級 的 態 勢 。

圖 二 、2024 -2027 年 全 球 九 大CSP 資 本 支 出 總 額 預 估(單位 :十 億 美 元)

資 料 來 源 ：TrendForce 、 宏 遠 投 顧 整 理

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31%  
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產 業 研 究 ， 近 期 超 大 型CSP 、
Tier -2 資 料 中 心 業 者 對NVIDIA GB/VR 等 整 櫃 式AI server 方 案 的
採 購 意 願 明 顯 提 高 ， 且Google 、AWS 新 一 代 ASIC 平 台 於 2026
下 半 年 陸 續 放 量，加 上 中 系 業 者 擴 大 採 用 本 地AI 方 案 以 滿 足 雲 端
大 型 語 言 模 型(LLM ) AI 需 求 ， 促 使TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出 貨 年 成 長 幅 度，從 原 先 的28% 更 新 為 近31% 。北 美 五 大
業者 2026 年 的 資 本 支 出 多 翻 倍 提 升 ， 反 映 出 大 型CSP 廠 持 續 擴
建 AI 資 料 中 心、GPU 叢 集、液 冷 散 熱 等 新 一 代 基 礎 設 施，以 應 對
生成式 AI 及 大 型 模 型 快 速 成 長 帶 來 的 算 力 需 求 。其中， Google 
2026 至 2027 年 都 將 側 重 自 家TPU 晶 片 發 展 ，2027 年 出 貨 量 有
望 繼 續 高 速 成 長 。AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主 力，自 研 ASIC 也 穩 定 出 貨，預 期 至2027 年 量 能 將 再 擴
大。 Meta 2026 年 主 要 採 用NVIDIA GB/VR 、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方 案，自 研 ASIC 布 局 預 計 至2027 年 將 明 顯 加 速，為 該 公 司 長 期
降低 AI 基 礎 設 施 成 本 、 提 升 推 論 效 率 的 重 要 策 略 。

3. 2Q26 PC 出貨量 年減 4.9% ，蘋果 市佔率逆勢擴張  
根據 國 際 數 據 資 訊(IDC )報 告 ，2026 年 第 二 季 全 球PC 出 貨 總 量 達
6,820 萬 台、年 減 4.9% ，主 要 由 於 記 憶 體 短 缺 持 續 推 升 零 組 件 成
本 ， 再 加 上 總 體 經 濟 環 境 不 佳 ， 導 致 終 端 需 求 與 換 機 意 願 大 幅 放
緩，整 體 拉 貨 動 能 無 力 延 續。其 中，Apple 2Q26 PC 出 貨 量 為670
萬 台 ，YoY +10.1% ， 主 要 受 惠 於MacBook Neo 帶 動 。 此 外 ， 面
對 零 組 件 短 缺 與 成 本 攀 升 逆 風 ， 蘋 果 憑 藉 龐 大 採 購 規 模 與 供 應 鏈
優 勢 穩 固 供 貨 ， 使 其 市 佔 率 逆 勢 擴 張 。

一、3Q26  AI 伺服器動能延續，筆電面臨 逆風  
公 司 公 告7 月 營 收 3399 億， MoM -6.3% ，YoY +120.7% ，今年
累 計 營 收2.21 兆， YoY +79.6% 。 展 望 3Q26 ， 公 司 預 期AI 伺服
器 營 收 持 續 強 勁 增 長 ， 但 因 第 二 季 基 期 已 拉 高 ， 季 增 幅 度 會 低 於
第 二 季 。此 外 ， 公 司 表 示 ，第 三 季 起 部 分 客 戶 專 案將由 Buy and Sell 
模 式 轉 為Consignment 模式，將 有 助 於 大 幅 減 輕 營 運 資 金 壓 力 並
舒 緩 毛 利 率 稀 釋影 響 。 筆 電 部 分 ， 由 於 主 要客 戶 已 於 上 半 年 提 前
拉 貨，再加上 記 憶 體 與CPU 成 本 大 漲，壓 抑 終 端 採 購 意 願，預 計
第 三 季 出 貨 量 將 季 減 逾20% 。 預估 3Q26 營收 1.05 兆， QoQ 
+1.5% ，YoY +112.5% ， 毛 利 率 5.1% ， 營 利 率 3.3% ， 稅 後 淨 利
288 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS 7.46 元。

全 年 營 收 雙 位 數 年 增 目 標 。筆 電 部 分 ，儘 管 上 半 年 連 續 兩 季 出 貨
表 現 優 於 預 期 ，但由 於 客 戶 提 前 備 貨且 缺 料 情 況在 下 半 年 未 見 明
顯改善 ，公司 預 估 下 半 年 出 貨 將 低 於 上 半 年 ， 全 年 筆 電 出 貨 量 預
估 將 較 去 年 呈 現 雙 位 數 下 滑 。而 營 利 率 部 分 ， 公 司 表 示 ， 由 於下
半年 AI 佔 比 持 續 提 升，有 助 於 產 品 組 合 優 化，預 估 全 年 利 潤 率 可

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
SEMICON Taiwan 2026
Takeaways
Key takeaways from CEO fireside chat, which included Hon Hai (HH) Chairman
Young Liu and Unimicron (UMTC) Chairman Shan-Chieh Chien:
• HH sees AI demand as  unprecedented in speed and scale, pressuring the
supply chain to expand capacity while raising capital intensity.
• Management views diversification as structural, accelerated by geopolitics
and countries' push for local production. We have written about this here,

here, and here.
• HH's strategy is "Made with Taiwan", exporting Taiwan's manufacturing IP,
system-integration know-how and execution speed to end markets via local
partnerships and production.
• UMTC said AI lifts ABF substrate demand and complexity (large sizes, higher
layer counts, more advanced designs), widening supply constraints. This
aligns with our view, which we have written here, here, and here.
• UMTC said it has held more than 10 meetings in the past 18 months with

suppliers and customers to align demand forecast and capacity, by actively
preparing capacity, preparing new facilities, securing land.
• Supply of upstream key materials, such as ABR and T-glass, remains tight.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 074: 260903_daiwa_ Laiyi

Source PDF: all report pdf/260903_daiwa_ Laiyi.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-03
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Consumer Discretionary

What’s new: We received an update from Lai Yih’s management after its 
upbeat 2Q26 results (see memo, 25 August). We are more confident in its 
revenue and operating profit returning to growth YoY in 2H26E and 2027E 
on new model launches from its key clients, a new client win and better 
seasonality approaching in 4Q26E, and thus, upgrade Lai Yih to Buy (1) 
from Hold (3).

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results key highlight: better-than-expected 
client and product mix. In 2Q26, we noticed that Lai Yih’s revenue and 
gross margin beat expectations on more favourable client and product mix. 
Its operating profit of TWD1.203bn (+566.6% QoQ and +8.7% YoY) beat 
our and Bloomberg consensus estimates by a respective 26.6% and 
41.1%. Lai Yih’s client mix for 2Q26 included Adidas (53.4%; -2.5% YoY), 
Hoka (36%; +9.0% YoY, record-high revenue mix level), Converse (7.8%; -
41.5% YoY) and New Balance (2.8%; -19.6% YoY).

Brighter 2H26E and 2027E outlook with rising new model launches 
from existing clients; we assume one new client in 4Q26. We believe 
3Q26E will be off-peak season for Lai Yih, but expect its revenue 
momentum to pick up QoQ in 4Q26E, along with gross margin expansion 
QoQ. Given the company’s track record with Adidas and Hoka, 
management noted it is in discussion with several potential new clients; we 
assume one new client to onboard in 4Q26. Lai Yih aims for 2027 
shipments to return to 2025 levels. Despite continuing inflation, the 
company targets to gain more high-end shoes with higher ASP to drive its 
overall ASP and gross margin expansion. Besides, Hoka will start to initiate 
new products in 2H26, which we believe will be positive to Lai Yih’s gross 
margin. Our 2026-27E EPS remain almost unchanged, and we introduce 
our 2028 forecasts, calling for EPS growth of 10% YoY.

What we recommend: We upgrade Lai Yih to Buy (1) from Hold (3) and 
raise our 12M TP to TWD230 (from TWD160) mainly on improving 
fundamentals expected from 2H26E. Due to a recovering earnings 
trajectory, we now use a target PER of 18x (from 15x), vs. its past-3-year 
range of 12-33x (average PER of 20x), applied to our 1-year forward EPS. 
We forecast an earnings CAGR of 18.5% over 2026-28 (previously -1.6% 
over 2025-27E). Downside risks: weaker-than-expected end demand; 
lower-than-expected market share gain; inflation.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 15-22% below the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more conservative on revenue and gross 
margin. However, we believe we are more positive on Lai Yih’s client win 
capability riding on the product mix optimisation trend.

3 September 2026

Upgrading: improving fundamentals

➢ 2Q26 results beat expectations in revenue and gross margin 
➢ Brighter order visibility in 2H26E and 2027E; new client in 2H26 
➢ Upgrading to Buy (1) from Hold (3); raising 12M TP to TWD230

Company profile 
Established in 1987, Lai Yih is a footwear manufacturer that collaborates with Adidas (56.4% of total 
revenue in 2025), Hoka (28.5%), Converse (12.4%) and Uniqlo. Its expertise lies in the production of 
high-quality vulcanised shoes, athletic footwear, cold pressed shoes, and specialised functional 
footwear. Lai Yih Group is headquartered in Taichung (Taiwan) and its production plants and 
development centres for clients are mainly based in Vietnam (91% of total shipment in 2025), Myanmar 
(7%) and Indonesia (1%).

Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
The CEO and chairperson of Lai Yih is not the same individual. We note that the CEO, Mr. Lin,
has 35 years of experience in this industry, which is a positive sign from an executive
perspective.
Capital management 1 Lai Yih's dividend payout ratio was 44-70% over 2022-25, which we see as rational due to its
capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 1 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was less than 1% of total revenue during
2022-25, and we note no suspicious activity.
G Supply chain
management 2
In supply chain management, the company emphasises product quality and safety. Due to its
focus on supply chain transparency, it has designated sales specialists to provide suppliers with
prompt responses to issues.
E Materials sourcing &
efficiency 2
For raw materials and energy usage, the company prioritises environmental sustainability. Its
production processes adhere to safety certifications and standardized procedures, while it strives
to improve energy efficiency and reduce pollution.

### Page 3

Hon Hai (2317.TW; Buy, on CL): Jul rev in line at +54% YoY; AI server and smartphones to support 3Q 
growth

Hon Hai’s Jul revenues were +54% YoY/ +15% MoM to NT$947m, or 2% higher than our 
estimates, overall in line. From a MoM perspective, Consumer Electronics & 
Components delivered significant growth MoM, benefitting from the pull-in 
momentum, followed by Cloud and Networking & Computing which achieved strong 
growth MoM. From a YoY perspective, all segments across Consumer Electronics, 
Components, Cloud and Networking & Computing delivered strong growth YoY, and 
management attributed this to the strong pull-in momentum and higher selling prices of 
Consumer Electronics and Computing products. We expect 3Q26E revenues to be +48% 
YoY / +22% QoQ to NT$3.1 trillion. June/ July delivered strong YoY (+52%/ 54% YoY), 
and we expect Aug revenues to remain strong at 73% YoY / 11% MoM to NT$1,051bn, 
supported by AI rack server ramp-up, and strong seasonality of the consumer 
electronics business. Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

### Page 4

Quanta (2382.TW, Neutral): AI server in strong ramp up; PC to decline in 2H26 on high memory cost

Quanta’s Jul revenue was down 5% QoQ / up 131% YoY, or 21% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong rack-level AI server shipments, along with the 
stronger than expected PC shipments at 4.5m units. Looking ahead, we expect 
sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on the memory price hike weighing on PC 
end demand. We model sequential revenue increases in Oct-Dec, supported by the next 
generation rack-level AI server shipments ramp up, which carries a higher ASP . Overall, 
we model Quanta’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 85% / 47% YoY, riding on the 
growing AI infrastructure trend. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral-rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$299 is based on an 
11x 2027E P/E, which references PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple 
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

### Page 6

AVC (3017.TW, Buy): Liquid cooling component ASP and GM upside on the rising complexity of server 
racks; July in line

AVC’s Jul revenue was up 57% YoY / 6% QoQ to NT$19bn, largely in line with our 
estimate. We attribute the strong YoY growth to the strong shipments of AI server liquid 
cooling components, riding on the increasing AI server end demand and a growing liquid 
cooling penetration rate. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
(1) rising shipments of next-generation rack-level AI servers, (2) an increase in liquid 
cooling dollar content in next-generation AI server racks given their growing complexity, 
and (3) a rising liquid cooling penetration rate in both ASIC and GPU AI servers. Maintain 
Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$3,986 on a target P/E multiple of 28.2x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

### Page 7

Wiwynn (6669.TW, Buy): July revenues sequentially increase; Capex strong increase shows strong AI 
demand

Wiwynn July revenues were in line with our estimates at NT$118bn, driven by solid 
general servers growth and its major ASIC customers’ new generation of ASIC AI servers 
(air-cooling) entering mass production since June 2026. Looking ahead, we model 
sequential revenues decline in Aug and Sep, considering the high-base of general 
servers, while liquid-cooled ASIC AI servers of its major customer, and GPU AI server 
racks ramp up is in 4Q26E. Nevertheless, the 3Q26 revenues growth would be much 
stronger than 1Q26 (-5% QoQ), and 2Q26 (+1% QoQ), up to +22% QoQ, and we expect 
further acceleration to +32% QoQ in 4Q26, driven by AI servers new models ramp up, AI 
servers expansion from ASIC to GPU, potential ASIC AI servers SKU expansion, and less 
impact of business model transition from Buy & Sell to Consign for general servers. 
Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$3,352.55. Our target price is 
based on 20x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

### Page 8

Wistron (3231.TW, Buy): July and 2Q26 beat; AI server rack in a strong ramp-up with customer 
expansion and specification upgrades

Wistron’s July revenue was up 61% YoY to NT$308bn, or 30% higher than our estimate, 
reflecting strong AI server rack demand from neo-cloud and enterprises, offsetting the 
impact of the model transition. We expect sequential revenue growth ahead through 
Dec 2026, driven by new GPU AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26, which 
will drive up the ASP , along with strong AI spending and the expansion of company’s 
customer base. The new GPU AI server racks have various racks across GPU, CPU, and 
LPU, supporting ODM growth in coming quarters. On PC, the company’s July shipments 
were at 1.6m units (-20% MoM / YoY), reflecting modest demand under high memory 
costs. Despite PC shipments likely being negative HoH considering the strong pull-in 
since late 2025, we expect the strong AI server rack ramp-up to offset the impact, 
driving 2H26 revenue up +28% HoH. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wistron 
Our 12-month target price of NT$281 is based on a 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived 
from PC/server peer EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a 
major factor for the stock’s share price performance.   
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected PC market recovery, 2) 
slower-than-expected AI server demand ramp-up, and 3) weaker-than-expected 
demand on general servers.

Gigabyte (2376.TW, Neutral): AI server in expansion; Strong capex commitment to capture the

Gigabyte’s Jul revenue was up 60% YoY / 3% MoM, or 18% higher than our estimate,

rack-level AI servers. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Sep, supported by the 
rising AI server shipments, and we expect revenues to stay high in 3Q / 4Q26E post 
strong growth in 1H26, driving 2H26 revenues growth at +15% HoH. Management 
remains positive on 2H26 growth driven by strong AI demand, customers’ faster AI 
deployment, and Gigabyte’s expanding customer base and enhancing services. 
Management expects their customers’ infrastructure readiness and components 
procurement to continue to improve in 2H26, supporting the procurement on AI servers. 
Maintain Neutral.

multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance.

recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

ASUS (2357.TW, Neutral): PC enjoying product mix upgrade; AI server client base expansion; July &

### Page 11

Compal (2324.TW, Neutral): AI server capacity in expansion; July better than expected

Compal’s Jul revenue was up 38% YoY / down 15% MoM to NT$81bn, or 10% higher 
than our estimate, reflecting AI servers shipment ramp up. Jul PC shipments was down 
22% YoY / 25% MoM to 1.8m units, reflecting the impact of 1H26 demand pull-in amid 
the rising memory cost. We model sequential revenue growth in Aug-Dec, considering 
the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, and PC product 
mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect Compal’s 3Q / 
4Q26E revenues to grow at 6% / 10% QoQ, benefiting from the rising AI server end 
demand from Neocloud clients. We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, 
supported by global capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, and Vietnam, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, which demand 
could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain Neutral on 
fair valuation.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$41.3. Our target 
price is based on 13.1x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

### Page 13

Inventec (2356.TW, Neutral): AI server supporting growth ahead; Memory cost weighing on PC 
shipments; Jul Rev and 2Q26 beat

Inventec’s Jul revenue was up 66% YoY / down 12% MoM, or 32% higher than our 
estimate, which we attribute to the strong AI server end demand, while the MoM decline 
was mainly due to a decline in PC shipments, given the demand pull-in in 1H26. We 
expect sequential revenue decline in Aug-Sep, mainly on a decline in PC shipments from 
a consumption pull in amid rising memory costs. Meanwhile, growth momentum should 
recover in Oct-Dec, supported by AI server shipment ramp up. Overall, we expect the 
company to deliver double-digit YoY revenue growth in 3Q / 4Q26E, on rising AI server 
end demand and Inventec’s capacity expansion. We remain positive on Inventec’s AI 
server business expansion, while the rising memory cost would cap the PC shipments 
growth ahead. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Inventec  
We are Neutral-rated on Inventec with a 12-month target price of NT$56.0. Our target 
price is based on 14.6x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers.

### Page 14

Pegatron (4938.TW, Sell): AI server driving 2H26 growth; PC growth capped by rising memory cost; Sell

Pegatron’s Jul revenue was +12% YoY / -6% MoM to NT$86bn, and we attribute the 
MoM decline to the sluggish PC end demand post the demand pull-in in 1H26, causing 
Pegatron’s PC shipments to decline to 0.40m units in Jul from 0.65m units in Jun. We 
model double digit MoM revenue growth in Aug-Sep, considering the low base in Jul, 
followed by modest sequential growth in Oct to Dec, mainly driven by the strong AI 
server shipments growth. Management remains positive on the momentum of AI server 
business in 2H26E, supported by the strong end demand from Neocloud and CSP clients. 
Overall, we expect Pegatron’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at +20% / +27% YoY. We are 
positive on Pegatron’s AI server business expansion, while we expect the tight memory 
supply to cap the consumer electronics growth. Maintain Sell.

Earnings revision: Pegatron’s 2Q26 revenue, GM, and Opex ratio are largely in line with 
our estimate, while 2Q26 non-op income is higher than expected, leading 2Q26 net 
income 22% higher than our estimate. We factor in Pegatron’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 
result and raise our 2026-28E net incomes by 10% / 8% / 7%, mainly on higher revenues 
and lower opex ratios. We raise our 2026-28E revenues by 5% / 5% / 4%, mainly on 
higher contribution from AI server business. Our 2026-28E opex ratios are slightly 
revised up, mainly on higher R&D expense to support the AI server specification 
upgrades and PC product mix upgrade towards AI PC.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Elite Material
Valuation
Our target price for EMC is set at NT$6,000, based on a target PE multiple of 30x on our 2027E EPS average. We believe our
target PE multiple, at the peak of its forward PE average in the past 5 years, is justified by a strong earnings outlook supported
by its leading position in CCL supply for AI GPU and ASIC (high m/s) from 2026E onwards. We also believe the continued spec

upgrade for CCL and price hikes should help support its margin expansion going forward. At our target price, the shares would
trade at 23.6x/13.2x 2026E/27E PB.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates EMC high-risk given high share price volatility, which we largely attribute to frequent debates over the
spec upgrade for next-gen AI servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope for aggressive spec
upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in

the end. However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth
potential among AI servers of different generations. As such, we do not assign a high-risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL
upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-expected AI server demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply chain (eg, glass, copper foil,

foundry or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in key AI server projects; and 5) stricter practice of OOC policy by end customers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

## Document 078: 260904_citi_yageo

Source PDF: all report pdf/260904_citi_yageo.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-04

### Page 1

•Broad-base 2Q, cost-capped margin. Mgmt. reiterated that 2Q26 growth 
was broad-based across every product line, end market and region, not a 
single AI pocket. GPM was slightly below Street, as raw material cost 
inflation offset part of the pricing and mix improvement. Internal 
manufacturing efficiency projects are underway to absorb it in the future.
•Jul price hike hits spot and direct. The Jul price adjustment covers spot 
prices and direct customers, vs. earlier rounds limited to contract 
customers, and spans c.50% of revenue including MLCC, tantalum and 
other capacitors. Spot pricing flows through immediately rather than at 
renewal, so mgmt. expects ASP and revenue effects shortly, with margin 
impact far more visible in 2H. Mgmt. also sees no sign of demand slowing 
down in the near-term.
•Tantalum now the largest line. AI is not an MLCC-only story for passive 
components. Tantalum grew fastest of any line in 2Q, suits AI power 
management, and has over taken magnetics to become Yageo's largest 
product line. MLCC also grew well and should keep growing in coming 
quarters. AI revenue is now 16% of total revenue, spread across tantalums, 
MLCCs, resistors and inductors rather than single product.
•65 sites, dual-region by product. Every product is manufactured in at least 
two regions, giving customers flexibility against tariff and geopolitical risks. 
Mgmt. believe the existing 65 facilities are flexible in adding new lines at a 
fast pace whenever shortages appear, saving customers 1-2 years vs. 
building from greenfield. The company also planned the capacity expansion 
ahead of demand.

### Page 2

Yageo (2327.TW)
04 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•YFIII and overseas MLCC bases. YFIII in Kaohsiung, commissioned in 2024, is Yageo's largest MLCC fab and still has
space for additional MLCC lines, with AI-specific equipment arriving monthly since last year. Outside Taiwan, Yageo
has been opening new lines within existing facilities in Mexico and China, leaving it able to absorb incremental AI
orders without a new building cycle.
•Lead times extend, LTA interest rises. Lead time of most of the products has lengthened vs. 1H26. Tantalum is the

tightest, followed by MLCC and resistors. Mgmt. indicated all AI-related lines are extremely tight, and more
customers are now approaching Yageo to sign LTAs.
•NP0 leadership on the power side. Yageo estimates a meaningful share (at least 30-40%) of NP0 high-voltage
MLCC, based on the internal assessment. Built on EV and industrial experience, NP0 fits AI's high-voltage, high-
temp duty and sits on the DC power side, while high-cap MLCC serves the near-chip stage. Yageo ships direct to

leading power supply players and is invited as first batch of design partners.
•Low-to-high conversion only partial. Converting low-end MLCC capacity to high-end is only partially possible.
High-spec MLCC stores more capacitance through higher layer counts, requiring advanced stackers rather than
conventional equipment. Yageo can optimize and redeploy some existing tooling, but not fully substitute, which is
why it is committing capex to new AI production line instead of relying on conversion.

Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 3

Yageo (2327.TW)
04 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Update
Accton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
August Sales +4% MoM/+59%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Accton's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• August sales were NT$40,873mn (+4% MoM/+59% YoY).
• QTD reached 66% of our 3Q26 estimate, at NT$120,883mn (+27% QoQ/
+66% YoY), and 70% of consensus, at NT$114,321mn (+20% QoQ/+57%
YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 revenue tracking in line with our estimates

• Our checks indicate that Accton has been negotiating with customers to let
them consign  those expensive components, which could weigh on its
revenue growth but should be positive to margins.
• Looking ahead, we expect the momentum to remain elevated into 2H26, as it
should benefit from broader-based migration to 800G network switches
from both existing and new customers, and the ramp of the new AI
accelerator model from its major customer should be in full swing.

• Stay OW with a PT of NT$3,500, based on 30x 2027 P/E.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW)
Base case, P/E, which we believe better captures the company's earnings growth momentum.
We assign a target P/E of 30x to our 2027 EPS estimate, which we view as justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 48% for 2025-28, based on our estimates. This implies a PEG of
around 0.6, vs. its average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for

an operating profit CAGR of 32%.

n Stronger data center capex
n Faster network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand losing share in AI networks
n Stronger custom AI accelerator growth
n Stronger ODM direct business model adoption
Risks to Downside
n Weaker data center capex
n Slower network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
n Softer custom AI accelerator growth
n Softer ODM direct business model adoption

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
August  Sales  +4% MoM/+156%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Chroma's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• August revenue was NT$5,632mn (+4% MoM/+156% YoY).
• QTD revenue reached 75% of MSe, at NT$14,772mn (+9% QoQ/+130% YoY),
and 79% of consensus, at NT$13,992mn (+3% QoQ/+118% YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 tracking ahead of market expectations and likely to deliver

over 20% QoQ.
• Looking ahead, we expect its power testing business to stay at an elevated
level, and the semiconductor & photonics business to continue growing HoH
into 2H26, driven by SLT, metrology, CPO and potential shipment of FT
handlers/burn-in ovens.
• Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 45x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Chenbro  | Asia Pacific
August Sales Flat MoM/+41%
Y oY
In this report, we focus on Chenbro's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August  sales were NT$2,457mn (flat MoM/+41% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 59% of our 3Q26 estimate at NT$8,368mn (+7% QoQ/
+48% YoY) and 56% of consensus at NT$8,763mn (+12% QoQ/+55% YoY).
Our view:
• We see its 3Q26 revenue tracking slightly behind our estimate.
• We think near-term sentiment should remain lackluster amid the temporary

hiccup resulting from supply chain management.
• Management remains optimistic on the 2H26 outlook. The demand from its
GPU/ASIC server clusters and general servers is strong, driving  orders for its
chassis and rack offerings.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chenbro (8210.TW)
Base case, derived from 27x 2027e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by the 33%
earnings CAGR we see for 2025-28, which implies a PEG of 0.8. This is in line the average of
0.8 over 2014-24, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 12x for a 15% earnings
CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger AI capex outlook
n Larger market share gains within existing customers

n Faster penetration into new customers
n Higher entry barrier among peers that leads to higher ASP/profitability
n Better macro outlook
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Less share gains within existing customers
n Slower penetration into new customers
n Intensifying competition leading to lower ASP/profitability
n Continuous macro uncertainties

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover . Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy , hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety , and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below .
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 106: 260909_daiwa_EPM

Source PDF: all report pdf/260909_daiwa_EPM.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-09
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: Eternal Precision Mechanics (EPM) reported its August 2026 
revenue after market hours on 9 September 2026.

What’s the impact: August revenue beat expectations. EPM reported 
August 2026 revenue of TWD359.8m, up 95.7% MoM and 111.5% YoY. 
QTD revenue reached 88.4% of our previous 3Q26 forecast and 74.2% of 
the Bloomberg consensus. Given the faster-than-expected revenue 
recognition schedule and a sharp increase in recognised high-end 
equipment units, which should drive a more favourable product mix, we 
raise our 3Q26 revenue forecast by 33% to TWD972.7m, gross margin by 
3.1ppts to 48.5%, and EPS by 92% to TWD1.86.

Vacuum laminators are emerging as the next ABF bottleneck. Despite 
a partial conversion of backlog into revenue in August, EPM’s backlog 
continued to increase from TWD810m at end-July to TWD880m in early 
September. With lead times extending further to 36 months, vacuum 
laminators are emerging as the next bottleneck in the ABF capacity 
expansion cycle. Given the robust demand from ABF substrate makers, we 
see upside potential to EPM’s capacity expansion plans. On margins, we 
expect continued product-mix improvement and the full realisation of price 
increases to lift gross margin by 6.6ppts/1.3ppts YoY to 53.2%/54.5% in 
2027/28E, driving a 2026-28E EPS CAGR of 141%.

Moulding film adoption opens a new growth avenue. Traditional 
transfer and compression moulding typically use granular, powder, or liquid-
type compounds for encapsulation. During our visit to SEMICON, Japanese 
material suppliers Nagase (8012 JP, JPY1,150.5, Outperform [2]; covered 
by Masayuki Nagano) and Resonac (4004 JP, JPY16,070, Outperform [2]; 
covered by Hidemitsu Umebayashi) noted growing customer interest in 
moulding films. These offer better filling performance, better warpage 
control and higher flowability. This makes them increasingly suitable for 
advanced packaging applications such as panel level packaging and 
component-embedded substrates. EPM supplies moulding-film laminators 
for both applications, with revenue contribution expected to begin in 2027.

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 22-26% to reflect the 
full realisation of price increases and improving product mix. We reaffirm 
our Buy (1) rating and raise our 12-month TP to TWD720 (from TWD600) 
based on an unchanged target PER of 40x, applied to our 1-year forward 
EPS forecast. Key downside risk: slower-than-expected substrate capex 
growth.

How we differ: We likely conduct a more comprehensive analysis than 
others on ABF industry demand and moulding film development.

9 September 2026

Company profile 
Eternal was established in 2022 through the spin-off of Eternal Material’s equipment division and its 
subsequent merger with Nikko-Materials, a wholly owned Japanese subsidiary. The company was listed 
in January 2026 and is the global leader in IC substrate vacuum laminators, serving all major substrate 
manufacturers worldwide.

### Page 5

5

Eternal Precision Mechanics (7795 TT): 9 September 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 26 Jun 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Eternal demonstrates a solid corporate governance structure supported by a balanced and
diversified board composition. The company’sboard consists of 7 members, including 3
independent directors, with backgrounds spanning industry operations, finance, accounting,
legal, and academia, enabling comprehensive oversight across business and governance
functions.
Capital management 2
Eternal adopts a prudent capital management strategy, supported by a stable shareholder
structure and disciplined financial policy. The company is majority-owned by Eternal Materials
(over 60% stake), providing long-term strategic backing and financial stability.
Related party &
transaction 1
Eternal discloses related-party transactions transparently and enforces strict governance
procedures to manage potential conflicts of interest. Board members are required to recuse
themselves from discussions and voting on matters involving personal or affiliated interests,
such as asset acquisitions, patent licensing, and remuneration decisions.
S Product quality &
safety 1
Eternal maintains strict product quality control, covering design, manufacturing, and delivery
processes. The company emphasizes equipment reliability and performance stability, supported
by long-term client relationships and low defect risks, ensuring high customer satisfaction and
consistent product safety standards.
E Product design &
lifecycle management 2
Eternal focuses on enhancing product durability and energy efficiency through continuous R&D
innovation. Its equipment design emphasizes precision, extended lifecycle and reduced material
waste, aligning product development with resource efficiency and long-term environmental
sustainability goals.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 2

iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max: (1) Cooling: the new iPhone is embedded with a more 
powerful SoC (A20 Pro) and Apple Intelligence, making thermal management essential 
to the device. The next generation of Vapor Chamber is used in the new iPhones, which 
we see as positive for the cooling suppliers. Read more: AVC, AAC; (2) Camera: variable 
aperture is adopted in rear cameras, in which lens aperture changes based on the focal 
length, allowing easier user control of DoF (depth of field), taking pictures in low lighting, 
or having a more natural broken gradient background when in portrait mode. Read 
more: Largan. Selling price is higher by US$100 (or high single-digit %) compared to 
iPhone 17 Pro series, with the same delivery time.  
Apple Watch and Airpods: Airpods 5 feature active noise cancellation, and are 
designed to allow more natural interaction (e.g. transparency mode, better conversation 
awareness). The wireless charging case models are US$30 cheaper compared to Airpods 
4. Apple Watch 12 and Ultra 4 are priced the same as the previous generation with 
better specifications: health sensing system with the most accurate heart rate sensing in 
a wearable, Siri recap, audio intelligence, etc.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

## Document 112: 260910_gs_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/260910_gs_TSMC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-10
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Presenters: Wendell Huang, SVP & CFO  
Bottom Line: We have three key takeaways: (1) TSMC continues to view AI as a 
multi-year megatrend that remains at a very early stage, with lower compute costs 
expected to accelerate AI adoption and ultimately drive greater demand for 
advanced silicon; (2) execution in Arizona continues to progress well, with Phase 1 
achieving yields comparable to Taiwan and already generating profits, while TSMC 
expects approximately 30% of its N2-and-beyond capacity to be located outside 
Taiwan in five years, primarily in Arizona; and (3) despite dilution from the N2 ramp 
and overseas expansion, management remains comfortable achieving its long-term 
gross margin target of 56% and above, supported by disciplined capacity planning, 
high utilization, productivity improvements, cost reductions, and pricing that reflects 
TSMC’s value. 
Key Takeaways

AI remains in the early stages of a multi-year megatrend: TSMC continues to n
see increasing adoption of AI. Management also highlighted the shift from 
generative AI toward agentic AI, which should broaden compute demand 
beyond accelerators to CPUs. TSMC remains confident in the durability of the AI 
cycle based on discussions not only with its direct customers but also with its 
customers’ customers. Recent visits of US CSPs further reinforced management’s 
confidence, as cloud service providers appeared well prepared in areas including 
power and broader infrastructure deployment. 
Lower compute costs should drive greater consumption and silicon demand: n
Management believes open-source models and continued improvements in 
computing efficiency will reduce token costs, leading to greater AI 
consumption and broader adoption, supporting incremental demand for 
advanced silicon. TSMC has already incorporated this dynamic into its capacity 
planning and continues to expand aggressively to support what it views as

### Page 2

structural, multi-year demand growth.
Disciplined demand validation remains critical amid aggressive expansion: n
TSMC uses a combination of top-down and bottom-up analysis to validate demand.
Its top-down assessment considers macroeconomic conditions, semiconductor
demand, fabless/system companies, the foundry industry, etc, while its bottom-up
approach evaluates demand across different segments and incorporates feedback
from customers and customers’ customers. Management acknowledged that

semiconductors will remain cyclical, but believes correctly identifying the underlying
megatrend is more important than attempting to forecast every short-term
fluctuation.
Arizona execution is demonstrating that TSMC’s manufacturing model can n
travel: TSMC reiterated that overseas expansion is based on customer demand for
geographic flexibility and an appropriate level of government support. Arizona
Phase 1, using N4 technology, is in operation, with yields comparable to Taiwan,

satisfactory quality and reliability, and positive profitability. Management views
this as an important milestone because it demonstrates internally and to customers
that TSMC can produce wafers in the US with the same quality as in Taiwan.
Equipment move-in for Phase 2, which will use N3 technology, is expected to begin
soon; construction of Phase 3 has started; and preparation work for Phase 4 and the
first US advanced-packaging facility is underway.

Arizona has substantial room for further expansion: In addition to its original n
Arizona development, TSMC has acquired another parcel of land capable of
accommodating an additional five to six fabs, supporting its recently
announced incremental US$100bn investment. Based on its current plan, TSMC
expects approximately 30% of its N2-and-beyond capacity to be located outside
Taiwan in five years, primarily in Arizona. However, the most advanced technologies

will continue to ramp first in Taiwan because the early-stage technology transfer
requires close collaboration and frequent movement of engineers among R&D and
manufacturing sites. Overseas deployment will generally follow after the technology
has stabilized in Taiwan.
Customers increasingly value and are willing to pay for geographic flexibility: n
Management noted that customers initially requested only a modest amount of
geographically diversified supply, but their requirements have increased as TSMC has

demonstrated successful execution in Arizona. TSMC believes this geographic
flexibility represents incremental value and intends to reflect that value in its pricing,
with customers increasingly willing to recognize the associated premium.
Technology, manufacturing excellence, capacity, and trust underpin TSMC’s n
competitive moat: Management believes customers select a foundry partner based
on access to the best technology, the ability to ramp rapidly into high-volume

manufacturing with attractive economics, sufficient capacity, and confidence that
the foundry will not compete with its customers. TSMC is confident in maintaining
this lead by continuing to advance its roadmap. Management also highlighted its
record of rapidly ramping new technologies, the scale of its wafer and
advanced-packaging investments, and its consistent delivery against customer
10 September 2026   2
Goldman Sachs TSMC (2330.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

commitments as key elements of customer trust that cannot be replicated through
CapEx or engineering recruitment alone.
Pricing remains strategic rather than opportunistic: TSMC does not intend to raise n
prices simply because near-term utilization is high, nor reduce prices simply because
utilization temporarily weakens. Instead, the company’s approach is to price
sustainably and consistently based on the value it provides, while ensuring that
customers can remain successful over the long term. Management noted that

TSMC’s structural profitability has continued to improve, with its long-term gross
margin target increasing from 50% five years ago to 53% and now 56% and above.
Leading-edge wafer and advanced-packaging capacity remain tight: n
Management continues to build capacity wherever possible to meet customer
demand. N3 demand remains particularly strong: unlike prior technology cycles,
when demand for a node typically began declining two to three years after its

introduction, TSMC is adding further N3 capacity and still does not expect supply
to be sufficient. The company is also expanding advanced-packaging capacity to
address strong accelerator demand. Outside TSMC, management identified power
and memory as potential bottlenecks cited by other parts of the AI supply chain,
while emphasizing that foundry capacity itself remains difficult to expand because
fabs require two to three years to build and another one to two years to ramp.

TSMC’s disclosed AI revenue understates its broader exposure: Management n
reiterated that its disclosed AI revenue definition is relatively narrow and primarily
captures GPUs, custom AI accelerators and memory base die. It excludes CPUs and
networking products because TSMC cannot reliably determine whether each chip is
ultimately deployed in an AI system. As agentic AI drives incremental demand across
x86, Arm, and RISC-V CPUs, as well as networking and other supporting silicon,

TSMC’s total AI-related exposure is therefore broader than its reported
AI-accelerator revenue. The company intends to retain its existing definition until it
can more accurately distinguish the end use of these products.
No visible barrier to continued transistor scaling: TSMC is currently ramping N2, n
with A14 expected approximately two years later, while development of the
subsequent node is already underway. Management does not currently see a

technical issue preventing delivery of the next node or continued advancement
beyond the existing roadmap. TSMC views close collaboration with customers as a
key foundry advantage, allowing the company to develop each technology against
specific customer performance, power, density, and economic requirements.
Cross-node capacity optimization is an underappreciated advantage: TSMC n
highlighted its ability to generate additional output through productivity

improvements and to convert tools and capacity across technology nodes. For
example, the company can use portions of its N5 capacity to support N3 demand
and can also optimize capacity across less-adjacent nodes where technically
feasible. Management views this cross-node flexibility as a distinctive element of
TSMC’s manufacturing excellence, allowing the company to respond more
quickly to demand changes while reducing the risk of capacity obsolescence.

Advanced packaging is strategic primarily because it enables wafer growth: n
10 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs TSMC (2330.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

CoWoS capacity remains tight, and TSMC already started outsourcing selected 
packaging processes to OSAT partners to support additional growth. Management 
emphasized that advanced packaging is intended primarily to ensure and enable 
wafer demand rather than serve as a standalone profit pool. TSMC is therefore 
developing multiple packaging architectures, including panel-level packaging, etc, 
while remaining open to customers using alternative packaging providers, as either 
approach ultimately supports demand for TSMC wafers. 
Advanced-packaging execution remains a key differentiator: TSMC noted that its n
current advanced-packaging capability supports approximately 5.5x reticle size, 
with a roadmap to reach approximately 14x reticle sizes within the next several 
years. While competing roadmaps may also appear attractive, management believes 
execution and the ability to deliver in high-volume manufacturing will ultimately 
determine competitive outcomes. TSMC does not believe competitors can use 
advanced-packaging wins to meaningfully displace its leading-edge wafer 
business, as foundry and packaging remain fundamentally different businesses. 
A unified TSMC culture supports overseas manufacturing execution: TSMC’s n
objective is not to create separate Taiwan and US cultures, but to establish a 
consistent TSMC culture centered on integrity, commitment, innovation, and 
customer trust (ICIC). Locally hired Arizona employees spend six to eighteen months 
training and working in Taiwan, while experienced Taiwan-based employees are 
assigned to Arizona to work alongside local teams. Over time, the number of Taiwan 
assignees should decline as locally trained employees assume greater responsibility. 
TSMC is applying this approach across its overseas manufacturing sites. 
Management remains comfortable with its 56%-and-above long-term gross n
margin target: TSMC reiterated its 6 major drivers of profitability: new-technology 
ramp, pricing, cost, product mix, utilization, and FX. The N2 ramp is expected to 
dilute 2026 GM by approximately 2-3ppts, but its profitability should improve over 
time toward the corporate average; management also noted that N2’s structural 
profitability is better than N3 at a comparable stage. Overseas expansion is 
expected to dilute GM by 2-3ppts during the early portion of the current five-year 
period, then widening to 3-ppts later as more overseas fabs begin production. Each 
new fab will initially create dilution before improving along its learning curve. Taking 
these puts and takes together, management remains very comfortable that 
disciplined capacity planning, healthy utilization, cost reduction, productivity 
gains, and pricing that reflects TSMC’s value will enable the company to deliver 
for shareholders and achieve its long-term gross margin target ‘of 56% and 
higher’ with focus on the ‘higher’ side. 
Valuation/Risks

TSMC (Buy): Our 12-month price target is NT$3,100, which is derived by applying a 
target P/E multiple of 22x to our 2027E EPS. Our 22x target P/E is benchmarked against 
1.0stdv above its 5-year trading average. For the ADR (TSM), we have a 12-month price 
target of US$620, based on a USD/TWD rate of 30.0 and an ADR premium of 20%. Key

downside risks to our views: (1) further deterioration in end-demand recovery

yields/execution resulting in worse-than-expected profitability; (5) stronger 
competition resulting in ASP/profitability erosion; and (6) unfavorable FX trend or 
higher-than-expected cost increase weighing on the margin outlook.
